ANSWER:SCĪPIŌ (NĀSĪCA) [ACCEPT “NĀSĪCA” ALONE BEFORE IT IS READ] BONUS: Scipio Nasica Serapio — the younger of the two — was shuffled off the stage of history in 132 B.C. when he was sent on embassy to what city, which Rome had peacefully acquired the previous year? ANSWER:PERGAMUM / PERGAMON BONUS: Scipio Nasica Corculum — the elder of the two — was the son of a prominent general during the Second Punic War. Name this man and his brother, who both perished at the battle of Upper Baetis in 211 B.C.? ANSWER:PUBLIUS SCIPIO and GNAEUS SCIPIO 
After ordering a massacre of ardent chariot racing fans in Thessalonica, what emperor served the public penance ordered by Bishop Ambrose, who may have convinced him to outlaw the Olympic games and paganism? ANSWER:THEODOSIUS {I // THE GREAT} BONUS: Ambrose also convinced the emperor Gratian to remove what object from the Cūria? ANSWER:ALTAR OF VICTORY // ĀRA VICTŌRIAE BONUS: What half-Vandalic general was left in effective control of the western empire upon Theodosius’s death in 395 A.D.? ANSWER:STILICHO 
During what war did an 11-day march by Marcius Philippus make up for Publius Crassus’s initial defeat and lead to the 168 B.C. battle of Pydna against Perseus, which forever ended the power of the kingdom once ruled by Alexander the Great? ANSWER:THIRD MACEDONIAN WAR BONUS: What region, which had once been ruled by Pyrrhus, did Aemilius Paullus order to be devastated by Roman troops following the defeat of Perseus? ANSWER:EPIRUS BONUS: At what battle did Publius Crassus suffer this initial defeat in 171 B.C.? ANSWER:LARISSA / CALLINICUS [DO NOT ACCEPT “CALLICINUS”] 
Aulus Postumius Tubertus won a victory at what site in 431 B.C., almost 30 years after a battle at the same site where Minucius Esquilinus was saved from the Aequi by Cincinnatus? ANSWER:(MT.) ALGIDUS [ACCEPT “ALGIDUS PASS”] BONUS: What priests, imported from the Aequi by Ancus Marcius, threw spears into enemy territory to formally declare war? MAELIUS

SEPTIMIUS SEVERUS BONUS: Early in Severus’s reign, he overhauled the Praetorian Guard, perhaps because of what prefect, who had presided over Didius Julianus’s bidding war and had conspired with Eclectus and Marcia to kill Commodus? ANSWER:(Q. AEMILIUS) LAETUS BONUS: What compromise did Septimius Severus make with one of his rivals, Clodius Albinus, so that he could focus first on defeating Pescennius Niger? A description is fine. ANSWER:DECLARED ALBINUS {CAESAR // JUNIOR EMPEROR} 
Which of the following provinces was furthest north: Belgica, Baetica, Asia, Aquitania? ANSWER:BELGICA BONUS: Which of those three provinces did not border the same body of water as the other three? ANSWER:ASIA BONUS: Which two of those provinces lie wholly or partially within the borders of the same modern country? ANSWER:BELGICA and AQUITANIA

A ruler of what kingdom described an approaching Roman force as “too few for an army, too many for an embassy” prior to a 69 B.C. battle in which Licinius Lucullus took his capital of Tigranocerta? ANSWER:(POPE) LEO {I // THE GREAT} BONUS: In the 420s A.D., Aetius burst onto the scene of history by leading a force of Huns in defense of what Italian usurper based at Ravenna? ANSWER:JOHN

ANSWER:PRAFECTUS URBĪ BONUS: Where was Flavius Sabinus when he was killed by the auxiliary troops of the German armies loyal to   Vitellius? ANSWER:ON THE CAPITOL(INE)

CRANTOR ANSWER: SKILLS

Which emperor took so much pride in his musical ability that he conducted a concert tour in Greece, and   firmly believed that the world lost a great artist with his death in 68 AD?  ANSWER: NERO BONUS: What future emperor fell asleep during one of Nero’s concerts, leading to his dismissal from his   entourage? ANSWER: VESPASIAN BONUS: Shortly before embarking on his tour, Nero uncovered a conspiracy that included what distinguished   general, whose victories over the Parthians in Armenia did nothing to spare him from execution? ANSWER: (CN. DOMITIUS) CORBULO 
The Romans lost 93 of their ships during what disastrous battle of 249 BC, which occurred after the   consul Claudius Pulcher foolishly threw his sacred chickens into the ocean? ANSWER:DREPANA BONUS: During which war did the battle at Drepana occur? ANSWER:1ST PUNIC (WAR) BONUS: Which Carthaginian commander, the father of Hannibal, took up the offensive in Sicily two years after   the battle at Drepana? ANSWER:HAMILCAR (BARCA)

ANSWER:IDAEUS BONUS: What two men stopped the duel between Hector and Ajax Telamon? ANSWER:TALTHYBIUS and IDAEUS

POMPEIUS STRABO

ANSWER: NYMPHIDIUS SABINUS BONUS: Nymphidius Sabinus previously helped Galba by spreading a rumor that Nero fled to what province,   whose prefects included Cornelius Gallus? ANSWER:EGYPT / AEGYPTUS BONUS: Nymphidius Sabinus was made Praetorian Prefect in the place of what man, who had been implicated in   the Pisonian Conspiracy and executed? ANSWER:FAENIUS RUFUS

ANSWER:ISCA BONUS: Which of the following is furthest south: Alalia, Carnuntum, Ephesus, Sabrata, Tarsus? ANSWER:SABRATA

ANSWER: AVIDIUS CASSIUS BONUS: Lucius Verus sent Avidius Cassius to the East along with what general, who focused his efforts on   invading Armenia? ANSWER: STATIUS PRISCUS BONUS: What Armenian capital did Statius Priscus capture and burn? ANSWER:ARTAXATA 
After its inhabitants successfully ensnared Hostilius Mancinus, what city negotiated a treaty with   Tiberius Gracchus, though this didn’t stop Scipio Aemilianus from destroying this city in 133 BC?  ANSWER: NUMANTIA BONUS: What kingdom, ruled at the time by Micipsa, provided a contingent to aid in Scipio’s siege of   Numantia? ANSWER:NUMIDIA BONUS: Mancinus took command in Spain from what consul of 141 BC, who was also besieged by Numantia’s   defenders and pocketed an indemnity they agreed to pay? ANSWER:(Q.) POMPEIUS

PETILIUS) CERIALIS BONUS: Who was this Batavian chieftain, who had previously been branded a traitor by Fonteius Capito? ANSWER: JULIUS CIVILIS BONUS: After hearing prophecies foretelling of their conquests, two chieftains of the Treviri entered secret terms   with Julius Civilis. Name one of them. ANSWER:JULIUS CLASSICUS or JULIUS TUTOR 
What man supposedly banned the use of iron weapons after capturing Rome, though Livy claims he   returned to Clusium after witnessing the bravery of his hostage Cloelia? ANSWER:LARS PORSENNA BONUS: At what battle of either 506 or 504 BC was a force led by Porsenna’s son Arruns repulsed? ANSWER:ARICIA

ANSWER: MAGNESIA (AD SIPYLUM) BONUS: At what location did Antiochus negotiate a treaty with Gnaeus Manlius Vulso after the battle of   Magnesia? ANSWER:APAMEA

ANSWER:(M. ULPIUS) TRAJAN(US) BONUS: Trajan’s conquest of Mesopotamia came with the aid of what Mauretanian lieutenant, who was later   executed by the Praetorian Prefect Attianus? ANSWER:LUSIUS QUIETUS BONUS: What other chief lieutenant crushed rebellions in Egypt and Cyrene, eventually replacing Quietus after   his execution? ANSWER:(Q. MARCIUS) TURBO

ANSWER: PHILIP THE ARAB // MARCUS JULIUS PHILIPPUS BONUS: Not devoid of its own conflict, Gordian III’s reign saw the revolt of what governor of Africa, which was   quickly suppressed by the governor of Mauretania? ANSWER: (M. ASINIUS) SABINIANUS
